## 🧩 D350 — V128-B2.1 · MISSION CONTROL WORKSPACE ADAPTER (`get_object_workspace`) — CANONICALIZED (2026-08-11)

> **Endpoint mới:** RULES **D350** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.38** · HANDOFF **V128-B2.1** · code HEAD **`be04f4b`** · migration tail **`20260811042807`**. Khối D349 phía trên = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT.
> **Provenance:** HANDOFF_V128_B2_1 (applied+verified) + live adapter body `pg_get_functiondef` + `aclexplode` + functional impersonation re-verify (phiên B2.2). **KHÔNG từ trí nhớ.**

**Mục tiêu:** một adapter **domain-agnostic** đọc registry (D349) + dispatch tới projector P0B, chuẩn hoá ra `WorkspaceProjectionDTO/v1` với **privacy chokepoint kép** (allowlist + forbidden-leak guard). Consume registry catalog; renderer đọc DTO, không đọc DB.

- **D350.1 — ⭐ `get_object_workspace(text,uuid,text)` = single privacy chokepoint.** SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, **gate order**: `is_admin` → `unknown_object_type` → `forbidden_object` (kind=forbidden ∨ projstat=none) → `not_available` (registered) → `scope_not_wired` (scope≠platform) → `reason_required` (privacy∈reason_required/restricted) → access-log → static CASE dispatch → allowlist filter → forbidden-leak RAISE → DTO/v1.
- **D350.2 — ⭐ Static CASE dispatch, KHÔNG dynamic SQL.** person→`admin_lookup_user`->'profile' · child→`admin_lookup_child`->'child' · media→`admin_lookup_media`->'media' · capsule→`admin_lookup_capsule`->'capsule' · ELSE `dispatch_missing`. Object-type KHÔNG bao giờ nội suy thành tên hàm (chống injection); thêm object = thêm CASE branch tường minh (proof B2.2).
- **D350.3 — ⭐ Privacy chokepoint kép (allowlist + forbidden).** `v_fields` = chỉ key ∈ `discovery_fields` → `ADAPTER_ALLOWLIST_VIOLATION` RAISE nếu lọt key lạ → `ADAPTER_FORBIDDEN_LEAK` RAISE nếu key ∈ `forbidden_groups`. Projector có thể trả rich payload (`admin_lookup_child` trả journal/evidence/capsules...) nhưng adapter CHỈ đọc `v_source = v_raw->'<identity_key>'` rồi strip về `discovery_fields` → sensitive groups sống ở top-level `v_raw` KHÁC mà adapter KHÔNG đọc. Privacy-by-source (D347.3) + adapter-allowlist = **double moat**.
- **D350.4 — Logger additive extension (capsule).** `admin_workspace_access_log` CHECK `('child','parent')` → +`('capsule')`; reason-required cho child/capsule; action `ADMIN_OPEN_CAPSULE_WORKSPACE`. Adapter gọi logger CHỈ khi `v_needs_reason` (privacy reason_required/restricted); open object (person/media) KHÔNG log. Fail-closed: log fail ⇒ `access_log_failed`, projection abort.
- **D350.5 — `WorkspaceProjectionDTO/v1` contract.** `{ok,dto,object_type,object_id,kind,scope,privacy_policy,projector_status,fields,capabilities,reason_logged}`. `fields`=identity(discovery), `capabilities`=registry `capability_vocab`. Hợp đồng data↔renderer; FE `ObjectWorkspaceModel` tiêu thụ (track FE riêng).
- **D350.6 — DB delta (verified live).** +1 fn (`get_object_workspace`) + logger body extended. 233→**234** fn · 222→**223** secdef · tables/pol/trg BẤT BIẾN (90/166/33). ACL cả 2 = `{authenticated,postgres,service_role}`, 0 anon/public (D15/D231). Migration tail `20260810150209` → `20260811042807`. Functional (impersonation): child/capsule reason-gated, media/person open, forbidden→`forbidden_object`, school(registered)→`not_available`, non-admin→`not_authorized`.

**Delta:** DB +1 fn + logger additive (mig `20260811042807`) · FE 0 · Edge 0. Inventory **90 · 234 · 223 · 166 · 33 · 1**. **Rollback = DROP `get_object_workspace` + restore logger child/parent-only.** Endpoint: RULES **D350** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.38** · HANDOFF **V128-B2.1** · code HEAD **`be04f4b`** · migration tail **`20260811042807`**.

Object cần context ⇒ qua legacy path **fail-closed** `context_invalid` (empty context không thoả required key). Backward-compat: object platform (không requirement) vẫn chạy qua legacy path như cũ.
- **D352.3 — ⭐ Registry gains `context_requirements jsonb` (registry-driven, nguyên tắc #7).** Mỗi object khai báo `{version, keys:{<key>:{type,required}}, allow_unknown}`. Default `{"version":1,"keys":{},"allow_unknown":false}` = 0 requirement (mọi object hiện hành trừ class). Requirement sống ở **data**, KHÔNG hardcode trong core — thêm requirement = UPDATE registry row, không sửa function.
- **D352.4 — ⭐ Validator `validate_mission_control_object_context(text,jsonb)` = data-binding validation, fail-closed.** SECURITY DEFINER `search_path=''` EXECUTE **internal-only `{postgres,service_role}`**. Đọc `context_requirements` từ registry → malformed req (`v_req` NULL / không object / `keys` không object / `allow_unknown` không boolean) hoặc context không object → `context_requirements_malformed` / `context_not_object`, `valid=false`. Type-check `uuid|text|int|bool` (`context_type_mismatch`, uuid qua regex + btrim normalize), required-key thiếu (`context_missing_required_key`), unknown-key khi `allow_unknown=false` (`context_unknown_key`). Trả `{valid,errors[],normalized_context}`; `normalized_context` CHỈ chứa key hợp lệ đã normalize. Validator VALIDATE **SHAPE**, KHÔNG cấp quyền. *(Mig `..._validator_errors_arrayfix`: `errors` field = `to_jsonb(text[])` — sửa xử lý mảng lỗi thành text[]→jsonb.)*

- **D352.6 — DB delta (verified live).** +3 fn (core + validator + 4-arg wrapper); 3-arg wrapper = `CREATE OR REPLACE` in-place (0 count) + registry `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN context_requirements` (tables bất biến). 235→**238** fn · 224→**227** secdef · tables/pol/trg/cron BẤT BIẾN (90/166/33/1). Core + validator ACL = `{postgres,service_role}` (0 anon/auth/PUBLIC); 2 wrapper ACL = `{authenticated,postgres,service_role}` (D15/D231). Migration tail `20260811080037`→`20260811110749`→`20260811111257` (2 mig: foundation + validator array-fix). `NOTIFY pgrst` (D289).

**Delta:** DB +3 fn + registry +1 column (mig `20260811110749` + `20260811111257`) · FE 0 · Edge 0 · Bunny 0. Inventory **90 · 238 · 227 · 166 · 33 · 1**. **Rollback = DROP core + validator + 4-arg wrapper → restore `get_object_workspace(text,uuid,text)` = B2.2 chokepoint body (inline logic, authenticated EXECUTE) → `ALTER TABLE DROP COLUMN context_requirements`.** Endpoint: RULES **D352** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.40** · HANDOFF **V128-B3.0** · code HEAD **`be04f4b`** · migration tail **`20260811111257`**.

## 🧩 D357 — V128-B6.1 · Task 5.2 · request_id INTENT LIFECYCLE (FE) + AGENT-MODE RE-FLOAT PRE-DEPLOY VERIFY — CANONICALIZED (2026-08-14)

> **Endpoint mới:** RULES **D357** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.45** · HANDOFF **V128-B6.1-Task-5.2** · **FE HEAD `e1c2ea8f`** · backend BẤT BIẾN (0 migration / 0 RPC / 0 schema). Khối D356 phía trên = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT.
> *(Đánh số D357/v1.45 = kế tiếp tip on-disk D356/v1.44. Nếu bản live đã vượt D356 do milestone khác, renumber theo tip thật.)*

**Mục tiêu:** Fix vòng đời `request_id` của Mission Control CLASS assign action (FE-only, 1 file hook), giữ idempotency mà không để replay nuốt intent mới. Kèm codify sự cố **agent-mode dependency re-float** thành rule pre-deploy verify. Milestone APPLIED + QA 4/4 PASS + DEPLOYED — ALL PASS.

- **D357.1 — request_id intent lifecycle (FE, sole owner = hook).** `src/features/mission-control/class/hooks/useAssignClassAction.ts` là owner DUY NHẤT của `request_id` phía FE (adapter/RPC forward 1:1, không sinh/không cache). Thêm `lastIntentRef` cạnh `requestIdRef`. `submit`: build payload TRƯỚC → `intentKey = JSON.stringify(built.payload)` → mint `crypto.randomUUID()` mới ⇔ `requestIdRef===null` **HOẶC** `lastIntentRef !== intentKey`; else reuse; lưu id+key cùng nhau. SUCCESS → clear cả 2 ref; `onError` + business-failure (`ok===false`) → GIỮ cả 2; `resetRequest` (nút Đóng drawer) → clear cả 2.
- **D357.2 — ⭐ Idempotency invariant giữ nguyên.** Same business intent (payload y hệt) ⇒ same `request_id` ⇒ backend replay path nguyên vẹn. Different intent (đổi `program_id`/`lead_teacher_id`) ⇒ fresh `request_id` ⇒ backend xử lý như thao tác mới (KHÔNG replay lỗi cũ). So sánh chỉ với intent submit **gần nhất** (không map lịch sử) → đúng cửa sổ idempotency. Contract frozen `execute_mission_control_action(p_action_key,p_object_id,p_context,p_input,p_request_id)` KHÔNG đổi.
- **D357.3 — ⭐ Agent-mode dependency re-float · pre-deploy tooling-pin verify (BẮT BUỘC).** Áp code bằng agent (`send_message`/"tự áp") khiến Lovable re-float `@lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config` `2.8.5→2.12.0` ở **package.json + bun.lock** (manifest + resolution) → `assert-tooling-governance.mjs` fail-closed **G1+G3** → build prod (`node scripts/assert-tooling-governance.mjs && vite build`) gãy (Lovable preview KHÔNG chạy gate, Cloudflare/`demenart.com` CÓ). **Sau MỌI agent edit, TRƯỚC deploy:** đọc TRỰC TIẾP package.json pin + bun.lock resolution — phải exact `2.8.5`. `get_diff` giấu lockfile (**D338**) → **`read_file bun.lock`**. Nếu re-float: restore **literal** package.json + bun.lock (manifest + resolution + URL region `europe-west1` + sha512 đúng version) từ commit canonical, **KHÔNG** non-frozen install. Prevention = paste-mode (D95); agent-mode = post-verify layer này. Pin canonical: `@lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config = 2.8.5`.
- **D357.4 — QA method: Request-blocking, KHÔNG Offline.** Mutation `execute` không reject khi DevTools Offline → request treo pending → hook không nhận `onError`, nút kẹt. Test terminal-failure/request_id phải dùng DevTools **Request blocking** (fail nhanh → `onError` → nút mở lại → retry được; request bị block KHÔNG tới backend → không tạo distribution → dropdown giữ nguyên). Pattern = URLPattern hợp lệ: `https://<ref>.supabase.co/rest/v1/rpc/execute_mission_control_action*` (đủ URL + `*` cuối, KHÔNG `*` đầu). Đọc `p_request_id` ở tab Payload.
- **D357.5 — Backlog non-goal (KHÔNG làm trong Task 5.2).** Mutation `execute` **thiếu client-side timeout/abort** → request treo (offline/mạng chậm) quay vô hạn không báo lỗi. Ứng viên ticket milestone sau (AbortController/timeout). Task 5.2 KHÔNG chạm.
- **D357.6 — QA verdict + deploy state.** QA 4/4 PASS (evidence `p_request_id` thật): A `0ecac41d…26526c1`×2 · B `c035d931…2027e669`≠A · C ledger `bc1f2b36…`≠`d20dc916…` · D `0bb20e70…2d14e`≠`1a41fc63…ef233b`. QA seed lớp `eeeeeeee-…-0001` @ Trường Demo Dế Mèn → **rollback sạch** (class+dist+lesson_sessions+ledger; live `total_classes=7`). Deploy `demenart.lovable.app` published tại **`e1c2ea8f`** (`is_published=true`). Deploy HEAD = `e1c2ea8f`, **KHÔNG** `b2ec6ad3` (commit re-float).

**Delta:** FE +`lastIntentRef` + reorder `submit` (1 file `useAssignClassAction.ts`) · pin restore `2.8.5` (package.json + bun.lock manifest+resolution) · backend 0 · Edge 0 · Bunny 0. ## 🧩 D358 — V128-B6.1.5 · MISSION CONTROL ACTION FOUNDATION — CANONICAL RECONCILIATION (append-only; runtime ĐÃ TỒN TẠI trong live) (2026-08-14)

> **Endpoint mới:** RULES **D358** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.46** · HANDOFF **V128-B6.1.5** · backend migration tail **`20260813113400`** · FE main (pin `@lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config = 2.8.5`, action lifecycle hook). Khối D357 phía trên = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT.
> **⭐ Bản chất block này = RECONCILIATION-ONLY.** Ghi nhận **Action Foundation ĐÃ SỐNG trong production** (backend batch Aug-13 · FE work Aug-14) nhưng **chưa được canonical hoá** — STEP 0 audit của B6.2 phát hiện backend + FE đều vượt canonical on-disk (D357/v1.45 dừng ở tail Aug-12 90/241/230). Block này đóng drift đó. **KHÔNG build · KHÔNG migration · KHÔNG mô tả B6.2 future state.**
> **⚠️ Chronology inversion (append-only):** D-number theo thứ tự **DOCUMENTATION**, KHÔNG theo thứ tự build. Runtime foundation thực tế **straddle D357** (backend Aug-13 06:00–11:34 → Task 5.2 request_id fix Aug-13 18:11–19:32 = D357 → FE foundation edits Aug-14 02:46–03:20). Vì append-only cấm renumber, foundation được ghi SAU D357 dù build TRƯỚC/quanh nó. Đây là ghi chú minh bạch bắt buộc.

**Mục tiêu:** Đồng bộ governance truth về đúng live runtime. Chỉ tài liệu hoá cái đang tồn tại; mọi hardening/generic-framework thuộc B6.2 → §D358.9 liệt kê DEFERRED tường minh (debt KHÔNG silently giải).

- **D358.1 — ⭐ Action layer = tầng vận hành thứ tư của Mission Control.** Chuỗi `Objects → Context → Actions → Memory`. Object Workspace (context seam D349–D356) giờ có thêm **Action surface**: discovery (`get_available_actions`/`get_mission_control_actions`), execution (`execute_mission_control_action`), memory (`get_mission_control_memory`), admin roll-up (`get_admin_action_center`). Tất cả live, wired cho object type **`class`** (object đầu tiên có action).

- **D358.2 — Action Registry (`mission_control_action_registry`) = descriptor catalog, registry-driven (nguyên tắc #7).** PK `id`; cột hiện hữu: `object_type · action_key · label · capability · risk_level · audit_event · status · metadata jsonb · created_at · updated_at`. RLS ON. **2 row active:** `class.assign` (label "Assign Teacher", risk `MEDIUM`, audit `CLASS_ASSIGNMENT_CREATED`) · `class.edit` (label "Edit Class", risk `LOW`, audit `CLASS_UPDATED`). Discovery `get_available_actions` đọc registry (`status='active'` theo `object_type`) sau khi validate context-match → trả `{action_key,label,risk_level}` ordered. **Registered-state của discovery = registry-driven THẬT.**

- **D358.3 — Execution Boundary (`execute_mission_control_action(p_action_key,p_object_id,p_context,p_input,p_request_id)`) = contract frozen (D357.2).** LANGUAGE plpgsql · **SECURITY INVOKER** (prosecdef=false) · `search_path=''` · owner `postgres` · EXECUTE `{authenticated,postgres,service_role}`. Gate order hiện tại: `auth.uid()` null → `MC_ACTION_PERMISSION_DENIED` · `current_profile()` null → `MC_ACTION_PERMISSION_DENIED` · **dispatch = literal `if p_action_key <> 'class.assign' → MC_ACTION_NOT_FOUND`** · null object/request → `MC_ACTION_INPUT_INVALID` · context phải object chỉ chứa `school_id` (else `MC_ACTION_CONTEXT_DENIED`) · input phải có `program_id` (+optional `lead_teacher_id`, else `MC_ACTION_INPUT_INVALID`) · class→school lookup (`MC_ACTION_OBJECT_NOT_FOUND`) · context.school_id ≠ class.school_id → `MC_ACTION_CONTEXT_DENIED`. Server validation boundary = **server-owned** (client không quyết định các gate này).

- **D358.4 — Domain Adapter foundation (`assign_class_distribution(p_class_id,p_program_id,p_lead_teacher_id)`) = class.assign executor.** SECURITY DEFINER · `search_path=''` · owner `postgres`. Chuỗi: class→school (`class_not_found`) → authz `is_admin() ∨ (current_profile_role ∈ {master_admin,sub_admin} ∧ school ∈ user_school_ids())` (`not_authorized_for_school`) → `has_subject_entitlement` (`subject_not_entitled`) → `dma_assignable_teacher_reason` (`lead_teacher_invalid`) → active-dup guard (`distribution_exists`) → INSERT `class_distributions(class_id,program_id,lead_teacher_id,applied_by=current_profile,applied_at=now)` → `write_audit_log('CLASS_ASSIGNMENT_CREATED', …)` → return `class_distribution_id`. execute RPC map các domain exception → `MC_ACTION_*` codes. **Đây là proto-adapter (một domain executor sau một dispatch literal), CHƯA phải generic adapter seam — xem D358.9.**

- **D358.5 — Action Request Ledger (`mission_control_action_requests`) = idempotency + lifecycle foundation.** Cột: `id · request_id · action_key · object_type · object_id · status · result_payload jsonb · error_code · actor_id · created_at · started_at · completed_at`. RLS ON, 3 policy (role `authenticated`): SELECT own (`actor_id=current_profile()`) · INSERT own-`processing` (CHECK `status='processing' ∧ result_payload NULL ∧ error_code NULL ∧ completed_at NULL`) · UPDATE finish-own (USING `own ∧ status='processing'`, CHECK `own ∧ status∈{completed,failed} ∧ completed_at NOT NULL ∧ result_payload NOT NULL ∧ (completed↔error NULL / failed↔error NOT NULL)`). execute RPC: INSERT `processing` `ON CONFLICT (request_id) DO NOTHING` → nếu conflict đọc existing: not-found→`MC_ACTION_REQUEST_CONFLICT`; mismatch `action_key/object_type/object_id`→`MC_ACTION_REQUEST_CONFLICT`; in-flight (`received/processing`)→`MC_ACTION_REQUEST_IN_PROGRESS` (`replayed=true`); else **replay stored `result_payload`** (`replayed=true`). Success→UPDATE `completed`+result; failure→UPDATE `failed`+error_code. **Idempotency theo `request_id` = THẬT** (safe replay), NHƯNG replay-conflict chỉ so `action_key+object_type+object_id` (không so input/context/actor) — xem D358.9.

- **D358.6 — Audit linkage + Memory projection = THẬT (không chỉ suy luận).** Audit event: `assign_class_distribution` gọi `write_audit_log` → row `audit_logs` (`action='CLASS_ASSIGNMENT_CREATED'`, `class_id`, `actor_id`, `metadata.program_id/lead_teacher_id/kind`). Memory: `get_mission_control_memory(object_type,object_id,limit,before)` (STABLE SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`) — **chỉ `object_type='class'`** (else `MC_MEMORY_UNSUPPORTED`), authz `is_admin ∨ (master/sub_admin ∧ school∈user_school_ids)`, đọc **`audit_logs WHERE class_id=…`** desc + cursor pagination (`limit+1`, `next_cursor`), map `CLASS_ASSIGNMENT_CREATED`→"Assigned <program>", join `profiles`(actor)+`programs`. `outcome` hardcode `'success'`. ⇒ **Topology `Audit Event → Memory Projection` = grounded trên `audit_logs`.**

- **D358.7 — FE lifecycle contract (main, `useAssignClassAction.ts`).** Hook = owner DUY NHẤT của `request_id` (adapter `missionControlAdapter.executeAction` forward `requestId→p_request_id` 1:1, không sinh/không cache). Đã có: **`lastIntentRef`** (intent lifecycle D357.1 — mint id mới ⇔ intent đổi; same intent ⇒ reuse ⇒ backend replay) · **`submitLockRef`** (duplicate-submit protection: khoá đồng bộ + `mutation.isPending`) · **`successNonce`** (success auto-close + stale-reopen protection: consumer track last-handled nonce, result persisted KHÔNG re-trigger auto-close khi mở lại drawer) · onSuccess→invalidate `workspace/actions/memory` query keys + clear cả 2 ref · onError/business-failure→giữ ref (retry idempotent). Conflict handling: business-failure (`ok===false`) giữ request identity để retry.

- **D358.8 — Inventory reconcile (live re-pin).** Canonical D357 ghi **90 · 241 · 230 · 166 · 33 · 1** (số B3.3, backend BẤT BIẾN theo Task 5.2 FE-only). **LIVE THẬT = `92 · 248 · 236 · 169 · 33 · 1`** · migration tail **`20260813113400`** (8 migration Aug-13: `20260813060038 → …060208 → …060625 → …063444 → …073711 → …073937 → …113248 → …113400`). Δ net = **+2 tables** (`mission_control_action_registry` + `mission_control_action_requests`) · **+7 functions** (action surface + helpers) · **+6 SECURITY DEFINER** · **+3 policies** (ledger RLS) · triggers/cron BẤT BIẾN. **Số 90/241/230 trong D357 (và các block trước) = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT; số hiện hành DUY NHẤT = block này.**

- **D358.9 — ⭐ Foundation state ≠ hardened; DEFERRED tường minh sang V128-B6.2 (debt KHÔNG silently giải).** Các điểm sau **là trạng thái hiện hữu**, KHÔNG phải thiết kế — ghi lại để B6.2 xử lý, KHÔNG xử lý ở đây:
  1. **Registry authority chưa enforce end-to-end:** discovery quảng bá `class.edit` (active) nhưng execute RPC literal-reject mọi `action_key ≠ 'class.assign'` (`MC_ACTION_NOT_FOUND`). Registry↔executor mismatch tồn tại.
  2. **Dispatch hardcoded, chưa có adapter seam:** execute = `if action_key == 'class.assign'`; registry **thiếu `adapter_key`** + `execution_mode/priority/group/availability/disabled_reason/required_context/input_schema/output_schema`.
  3. **Intent fingerprint chưa có:** ledger không có cột `intent_fingerprint`; replay-conflict chỉ so `action_key+object_type+object_id` (bỏ qua `input/context/actor`) ⇒ `MC_ACTION_INTENT_MISMATCH` hiện KHÔNG phát sinh được.
  4. **Ledger ownership chưa hardened:** execute RPC = INVOKER; policy `finish_own` cho `authenticated` set `result_payload` tuỳ ý (miễn shape status nhất quán) ⇒ forged-completed-ledger khả dĩ qua PostgREST trực tiếp.
  5. **FE class-specific:** chưa có generic action renderer/execution controller (hiện là `useAssignClassAction` + class drawer).
  6. **Memory single-domain:** chỉ `object_type='class'`; outcome hardcode `'success'`.
  Ba debt B3.3 (`subscription`/`support_case`/`privacy_request` — D356.13) VẪN registered-unwired, KHÔNG đụng ở đây.

**Delta:** DB **0** · FE **0** · Edge **0** · Bunny **0** — RECONCILIATION-ONLY (tài liệu hoá runtime có sẵn, KHÔNG mutation). Inventory (reconciled-to-live) **92 · 248 · 236 · 169 · 33 · 1**. **Rollback = xoá block D358 khỏi RULES** (không có artifact runtime nào để hoàn nguyên — block thuần tài liệu). ## 🧩 D359 — V128-B6.2-A1 · LEDGER FINALIZATION BOUNDARY (Governance Execution Integrity Hardening · G4 closed) — CANONICALIZED (2026-08-14)

> **Endpoint mới:** RULES **D359** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.47** · HANDOFF **V128-B6.2-A1** · backend migration tail **`20260814114948`** (`v128_b6_2_a1_ledger_finalization_boundary`) · FE main pin `2.8.5` (0 FE change). Khối D358 phía trên = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT.

Đóng gap **D358.9.4** (ledger forge). **KHÔNG** chạm D358.9.1/.2/.3/.5/.6 (registry-authority G1 · adapter-seam G2 · intent-fingerprint G3 · generic-renderer G5 · memory single-domain G6) — vẫn DEFERRED. ## 🗂️ D366 — V128-B9.2 · AUTHORITY RESOLVER FOUNDATION CONTRACT — CANONICAL RATIFICATION (governance only · 0 implementation) — CANONICALIZED (2026-08-16)

**Status:** V128-B9.2 **RATIFIED (governance)**. Canonicalizes the B9.1 design freeze (artifact `DMA_V128-B9.1_AUTHORITY_CONTRACT_DESIGN_FREEZE.md`) — frozen against RULES **D365** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.53** · HANDOFF **V128-B8-CLOSEOUT** · backend tail **`20260815182235`**; artifact STEP-0 lineage re-verified byte-for-endpoint **match** against live canonical (zero drift). This block is **governance canonicalization ONLY** — **0 implementation · 0 SQL · 0 migration · 0 DB mutation · 0 FE · 0 UI · 0 executable invariant · 0 enum minted · 0 constraint minted**. Backend tail **UNCHANGED** `20260815182235` (no migration). Authority Resolver = **design-frozen conceptual primitive, NOT built**. Read from real artifacts, not memory.

- **D366.1 — ⭐ Authority Resolver Foundation Contract (primitive definition, frozen).** The Authority Resolver is a **pure policy-decision primitive** answering exactly one question, deterministically and side-effect-free: *"Does this actor have authority to exercise this capability, on this object, in this context — and from which authority source?"* Properties: **read-only** (never mutates decision / business object / ledger / audit) · **deterministic** (same inputs → same verdict; no row-order/wall-clock/hidden-state dependence) · **explainable** (returns authority source + machine-readable reason codes, not just boolean) · **consumed-not-embedded** (callers invoke it; they do not re-derive authority). **Explicit rejections (what it is NOT):** simple role checker · permission-checker replacement · RLS replacement · adapter replacement · lifecycle-controller replacement. Design-frozen; **not implemented**.

- **D366.2 — ⭐ Authority model (frozen).** `Authority = WHO (actor identity) + WHAT (capability/action_key) + WHERE (organization scope) + ON WHICH OBJECT (resolved object context) + UNDER WHICH DECISION CONTEXT (self-approval prohibition · requester identity · decision phase)`. Authority is a **contextual verdict** — NOT a classification, NOT an entitlement. Frozen inequalities: **Role ≠ Authority** (role = input; necessary, never sufficient — `master_admin` of School A has no authority over School B's object) · **Permission ≠ Authority** (`profiles.permissions[]`/`has_permission()` = one *possible* authority source, currently 0/27 populated, unused by Mission Control) · **Capability ≠ Authority** (capability = the WHAT that exists; authority = WHO may exercise it here).

- **D366.3 — ⭐ capability-first input contract (frozen).** `capability`/`action_key` = **first-class PRIMARY** authority input. `decision_type` = **secondary/downstream context** — MUST NOT be the primary authority anchor. **Rationale (path symmetry):** capability lives in the action registry and is present in BOTH the direct-execute (MEDIUM → adapter, no decision wrapper) and decision-gated (HIGH+ → decision open→resolve→transition) paths; `decision_type` exists **only** in the HIGH+ path. If `decision_type` were primary, every direct-execute MEDIUM action would have **no authority anchor** — the resolver could not authorize the most common path. Conceptual signature (no API, no SQL, no implementation): `resolve_authority(actor, capability_or_action_key, object_context, decision_context) → { eligible, authority_source, reason_codes }`; `decision_context` optional — absent for direct-execute, present when a Decision lifecycle wraps the action.

- **D366.4 — ⭐ authority-policy boundary (single-source VERDICT; CTO Correction 1 applied).** The Authority Resolver is the **single deterministic source of the authority policy verdict** — **explicitly NOT "single source of authorization."** RLS (row visibility), adapter (domain invariants + transactional safety), and the lifecycle controller (`_mc_transition_decision`, B8 mutation boundary) **remain and continue to enforce**; authority policy ≠ complete enforcement. **Resolver OWNS:** authority policy decision · eligibility verdict · authority source · reason codes. **Resolver does NOT own:** RLS enforcement · domain invariant · transaction safety · lifecycle mutation. Adapter residual role/school checks, where retained, = **domain-safety defense-in-depth, NOT a parallel authority policy engine** (closes Risk 3 — policy divergence). RLS `object-not-found` = **visibility** outcome, ≠ **authority** verdict (D362.7 preserved; visibility net stays beneath the resolver). Migration direction (conceptual): authority *policy* centralizes into the resolver; enforcement *layers* remain but stop making independent authority decisions.

- **D366.5 — ⭐ identity model decision (Option A; enforcement deferred).** Frozen as a **design decision, not yet schema**: **one authenticated user → exactly one authority-bearing profile**. Delegation/representation are modeled as authority **sources** layered on a single identity — **never** as multiple profiles. Live state (recorded, not changed): `profiles.user_id` FK→`auth.users(id)`, **no UNIQUE constraint**; duplicate `user_id` = 0; max profiles/user = 1; helpers resolve via `LIMIT 1` (`current_profile()`); D93 invite/link flow already de-duplicates on email (operational intent already 1:1). Maps cleanly to `audit_logs.actor_id = profiles.id` (D88). **Implementation precondition (NOT done here):** enforcing `UNIQUE(user_id)` is an implementation step requiring a reconciliation pass — (a) re-verify duplicates still = 0 at implementation time, (b) confirm the D93 invite/link flow is not broken by the constraint. **No constraint minted; precondition recorded only.**

- **D366.6 — ⭐ controlled vocabulary DRAFT (reserved candidates; NOT minted — CTO Correction 2 applied).** B9.2 records **only** controlled/reserved vocabulary **DRAFT**. The final `authority_source` enum and `reason_code` taxonomy are **NOT minted here** — they are minted only **after B9.3 evidence**. Reserved candidate `authority_source` (illustrative, **non-canonical**): `platform_role` · `organization_role` · `explicit_permission` · `assignment` · `delegation` (future-only) · `none`. Reserved candidate `reason_codes` (illustrative, **non-canonical**): `actor_unresolved` · `object_context_mismatch` · `organization_scope_mismatch` · `role_not_eligible` · `capability_not_granted` · `self_decision_forbidden` · `authority_granted`. Output-contract principle retained: the verdict must be **deterministic, explainable, machine-readable** so no caller hardcodes its own exception strings. **⚠ Supersession (recorded):** B9.1 §8.7 recommended *minting* these enums at B9.2 as canonical vocabulary; **CTO ratification review SUPERSEDES that recommendation** — final taxonomy is deferred to B9.3 evidence. This D-rule is the authoritative disposition; B9.1 §8.7 stands as historical recommendation only.

- **D366.7 — adoption strategy (Strangler; frozen ordering).** Big-bang rewrite of all authority sites (≈51 `is_admin`, ≈29 hardcoded `master_admin/sub_admin`, ≈18 `same_school`) = **REJECTED** (maximal blast radius on live multi-tenant system; no rollback granularity; couples authority refactor with behavioral risk; violates contained-verifiable-step discipline). **Strangler, 4 phases:** Phase 1 — new governed actions authorize via resolver from birth (zero legacy behavior to preserve) → Phase 2 — Decision paths (open/inbox/resolve/cancel) consume resolver (highest authority duplication on smallest/most-contained surface; hard-centralizes self-approval prohibition, closes Risk 4) → Phase 3 — existing adapters migrate authority **policy** into resolver (domain-safety checks stay local) → Phase 4 — legacy admin RPCs migrate last (largest count, lowest decision-criticality). Each phase independently verifiable + rollback-able. **Authority-source contract frozen BEFORE Phase 3** — prevents Risk 1 (resolver degenerating into relocated `role IN (...)`): adapters migrate *into* a contract, not into an empty function.

- **D366.8 — ⭐ DISPOSITION + deferred boundary.** B9.1 design freeze **RATIFIED** (governance). Authority Resolver = **design-frozen conceptual contract; 0 implementation**. **No D-rule in this block mints an executable invariant, enum, constraint, function, trigger, policy, or migration.** Public inventory UNCHANGED; backend tail UNCHANGED `20260815182235`. **Deferred (NOT authorized by this block):** **B9.3 — Phase-1 resolver skeleton** (first implementation: resolver authorizes one *new* governed action end-to-end, verify matrix, zero legacy touch) · final `authority_source` enum + `reason_code` taxonomy mint (post-B9.3 evidence) · `UNIQUE(user_id)` enforcement (post reconciliation pass) · delegation · object-scope expansion (`child.transfer`, session/school) · multi-approver/quorum · notification · inbox UI · workflow builder · AI recommendation · HIGH/CRITICAL activation. **Await explicit CTO next-milestone (B9.3) authorization.**

**Delta:** **0 migration · 0 SQL · 0 DB mutation · 0 FE.** Governance-only canonical append (D366.1–D366.8). **Public inventory UNCHANGED (live baseline from D365/v1.53): tables 94 · fns 251 · secdef 239 · policies 169 · triggers 34 · cron 1**; `mc_internal` {7 fn / 6 secdef}. Governed path dormant (class.assign MEDIUM → auto). FE main pin `2.8.5`, 0 change. **Rollback: none required (no state change); to reverse this governance decision = append a superseding D-rule.** Endpoint: RULES **D366** · SYSTEM_MAP **v1.54** · HANDOFF **V128-B9.2-CANONICAL-RATIFICATION** · backend tail `20260815182235` · FE main pin `2.8.5`. Khối D365/v1.53 (B8-I1 Decision Lifecycle Foundation) = HISTORICAL SNAPSHOT (BẤT BIẾN).
